ucomisd %xmm1,%xmm0
je .someloop

### method jvzero(dt=DOUBLE, freg=xmm2, greg=r8, label=someloop, vreg1=zmm0, vreg2=zmm1)

### method jvzero(dt=HALF, freg=xmm2, greg=r8, label=someloop, vreg1=zmm0, vreg2=zmm1)

### method jvzero(dt=SINGLE, freg=xmm2, greg=r8, label=someloop, vreg1=zmm0, vreg2=zmm1)

vptest %zmm1,%zmm1
jne .someloop
